{"forms":[{"form":"mesostates","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An intermediate physical state of matter between solid and liquid, in which the molecules have more freedom of movement than when locked in a crystal lattice, but less freedom of movement than when in the liquid state."],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["A composite state of a physical system that is determined by the overall configuration of many microstates; e.g. the heat of a substance (mesostate), which is made up of the aggregate of motions of many molecules (microstate)."],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics","thermodynamics"]},{"glosses":["A description of an aspect of a geographical system at a level between complete detail (the microstate) and a simple summary statistic or percentage (the macrostate)."],"topics":["geography","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["An intermediate metabolic product in the formation of anastates or catastates."],"tags":["rare"],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences"]}],"upstream_line":4021629,"upstream_offset":9901973374,"upstream_record_sha256":"c8bf2ae5e0c26915e8d4e0e567f60e9d5da2a1201a39e58ff6084af82ba05543","word":"mesostate"}
